Thus final program can be extremely simple:
```python
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    # theoretical optimum 2; n=1 constant.
    # But maybe use parent to satisfy? Could check parent and return if lower? Cannot lower than 2 in exact, but due rounding maybe parent below 2? We can evaluate parent and if finite score <2 return parent? But parent length 3809 O(14M) okay. But if parent not available? try except.
    best = [1.0]
    best_score = evaluate_sequence(best)
    try:
       p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float).ravel()
       if p.size>0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
           cand = [float(max(0,min(1000,x))) for x in p]
           s = evaluate_sequence(cand)
           if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
               best = cand; best_score=s
    except NameError: pass
    return best
```

Let's design final code:
```python
import numpy as np
import time

def _score(seq):
    try:
        s = evaluate_sequence(seq)
        s = float(s)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    return s if np.isfinite(s) else np.inf

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 1300.0)
    fallback = [1.0]
    best_seq = fallback
    best_score = _score(best_seq)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        # If even fallback fails, return it anyway? But cannot. Maybe set 1.0.
        pass
    # Use parent if available and budget allows.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        try:
            parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        except Exception:
            parent = None
        if parent is not None and parent.size > 0 and np.isfinite(parent).all():
            # clip to evaluator domain
            cand = [float(min(1000.0, max(0.0, x))) for x in parent]
            s = _score(cand)
            if s < best_score:
                best_score, best_seq = s, cand
    # Optionally tiny numerical search n=2 around constant for below2? Could include.
    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```

Potential optimizer: Since objective is max of convolution coefficients, we can use a "spectral normalization" power iteration:
Given a, compute c. Active lags K where c close to max. We want reduce them. The dual variables y_k. Algorithm:
- While time:
  - c = conv(a) (FFT) real, normalized S=1.
  - Find P top lags (e.g., 10% or threshold max - τ*range). Use top P to average gradient: grad = (2/P) Σ_{k in top} shift(a, k). shift(a,k)_j = a[k-j] if valid else0.
  - To reduce max lags, update `u = a - lr * (grad - avg_grad)`? If subtract grad normalized? Since sum a fixed, subtracting proj: grad has mean? For top lags, mean of grad maybe 2*mean? Wait c_k=Σ a_i a_{k-i}; derivative wrt a_j sum_j =2c_k? Only for valid? Let's test n=2 c1=2ab; grad=[2b,2a], S=1 sum grad=2. Sum=2 not 2c1 (c1=2ab, if S=1, c1 not constant). Actually scale derivative: c_k(λa)=λ^2c, derivative wrt λ at1=2c. Sum_j j? ∑ grad_j * a_j^{?} no. Sum grad_j may not equal2c. For constant a with S=1, c_k varies; grad sum?     n=len(a); m=1; while m<2*n-1: m*=2 (or use scipy next_power)

```
def make_profile(n, p=0.5, q=0.0, alpha=1.0, beta=1.0, gamma=0.0):
   i=np.arange(n)
   a=(i+alpha)**(-p)
   if gamma!=0: a*=(1 - i/(n-1))**gamma # maybe ensure positive
   if q!=0: a*=(n-1-i+beta)**(-q)
   a=np.clip(a,1e-12, None)
   a/=a.sum()
```

```
    c=conv(a)
```

Implement LP row builder for active active K:

```
def build_A(a, K, n):
    rows=[]; cols=[]; data=[]
    for k in K:
        lo = max(0, k-n+1); hi = min(n-1, k)
        for j in range(lo, hi+1):
            p = k-j
            rows.append(k_index); cols.append(j); data.append(a[p])
    A = csr_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(P,n))
```

Computing c via FFT:
```
def conv_fft(a):
    n=len(a); m=1; while m < 2*n-1: m <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,m)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,m, m)
    return c[:2*n-1]
```
Since a real, fa*fa might have imaginary? Square not conjugate; multiplication of complex gives complex. Real part. For numerical, use `np.real`. Could use `irfft(fa*fa)`. Good. Convolution of real with itself real; imag small.

LP implementation:
```
def lp_step(a, M, P, target_sum, t_values):
    K = top P lags
    A = np.zeros((P,n))
    for r,k in enumerate(K):
       if k<n: A[r,:k+1]=a[k::-1]
       else: A[r,k-n+1:]=a[n-1:k-n:-1]  # check slice length
    res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(P,M), bounds=(0,None), method='highs')
    if not res.success: return None
    g=res.x
    sumg=g.sum()
    if sumg <=0: return None
    g = g*target_sum/sumg
    # Try mix fractions
    for t in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.8]:
       cand=(1-t)*a+t*g; normalize; if maxc < best: return cand,t
    return None
```

Can we implement a better deterministic iteration from "minimax quadratic" using proximal point?
Objective with sum fixed S0: minimize M = max c. We can solve via bisection on M and feasibility of c≤M. Feasibility: find a≥0, sum=S0, max a*a≤M. This is a nonconvex quadratic feasibility. But maybe can be relaxed by considering only active linear constraints? Not.

```
    # weight softmax with temperature temp = max((m-c.max())? )

    # If all close, weights uniform. Use exp((vals - m)*tau)

    w/=w.sum()
    grad=np.zeros(n)

          grad[:kk+1] += ww*a[kk::-1]
       else:
          st=kk-n+1
          grad[st:] += ww*a[n-1:st-1:-1]
    grad *= S0? no.
    return grad
```

```
def random_block(a, rng):
```

LP step:
We need import `scipy.optimize.linprog` at top maybe. Use if available and time. We can implement with dense active A. For n=2048, P=16 or 32. Let's set P=24. Function:
```
def lp_direction(a, M, P=24):
   idx=np.argsort(conv(a))[-P:]
   A=np.zeros((P,n))
   for r,k in enumerate(idx): fill
   c=np.ones(n); c*=-1
   try:
      res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(P,M), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'time_limit':5})
   except: return None
   if not res.success: return None
   g=res.x
   s=g.sum()
   if not finite or s<=0: return None
   g*=S0/s
   return g
```
But solving LP with time_limit option? HiGHS supports `options={'time_limit': ...}`? Maybe yes for `highs`. To be safe catch. We can set `options={'presolve': True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9}`. If time_limit not supported, could error. Use try with options without time_limit.

```
def conv_real(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    m=fft_len
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,m)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,m)
    c=c[:conv_out_len]
    c=np.real(c)
    # due sign, if min negative large? set small neg 0? Do not alter too much.
    return c
```

```
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog=None

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()

       return fallback
    S0=math.sqrt(2.0*float(n))
    conv_len=1
    while conv_len < 2*n-1: conv_len <<=1
    conv_out=2*n-1
    def conv(a): ...
    def score(a): ...
    def normalize(a): ...
    def make_profile(p, alpha, taper, add, sym): ...
    profiles=[]
    # generate with time check? We can just; but if many, time. Keep count.
    iArr=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    fnorm = n-1 if n>1 else 1
    count=0
    for p in (0.35,0.45,0.5,0.55,0.65,0.8):
      for alpha in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
        base=(iArr+alpha)**(-p)
        for taper in (0.0,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0):
          if taper: b=base*np.exp(-taper*iArr/fnorm)
          else: b=base
          for add in (0.0,0.1,0.25):

                if not time.monotonic()<deadline: break...

                count++
```

Mirror loop code more concrete:
```
best_surv = score(best_profile)
cur = best_profile.copy()
history=[]
P = max(16, min(64, (2*n)//100))  # ~76 for 3809, cap64.
mirror_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + max(20.0, (budget-60)*0.5))
eta=1.0
noimp=0
for it in range(200000):
   if time.monotonic() > mirror_deadline: break
   c=conv(cur); c=np.maximum(c,0)
   M=float(c.max())
   if not finite: break
   # top idx
   if (2*n-1) > P:
       idx=np.argpartition(c, -P)[-P:]
   else: idx=np.arange(2*n-1)
   vals=c[idx]
   # weights: closer to M heavier
   diff=M - vals
   smax=diff.max()
   if smax > 1e-12:
       w=np.exp(-diff/(0.1*smax))
    else: w=np.ones_like(vals)
   w/=w.sum()
   grad=np.zeros(n)
   for kk,ww in zip(idx,w):
      if ww==0: continue
      if kk < n:
         grad[:kk+1] += ww*cur[kk::-1]
      else:
         st=kk-n+1
         grad[st:] += ww*cur[n-1:st-1:-1]
   grad /= S0
   d=grad - grad.mean()
   scale=d.std()
   if scale < 1e-12:
      # try random
      noimp+=1
      if noimp>100: break
      continue
   eta_base=0.5/scale
   improved=False
   for factor in (1.0,0.3,2.0,0.1):
      eta=min(1000.0, max(1e-4, factor*eta_base))
      cand=normalize(cur*np.exp(-eta*d))
      s=score(cand)
      if finite and s < best_surv - 1e-10:
         cur=cand; best_surv=s; noimp=0; improved=True; break
   if not improved:
      noimp+=1
      # random exploration
      if noimp % 50 == 0:
         sig=0.05 + 0.2*min(1.0, noimp/500)
         cand=cur.copy(); width=int(2**rng.integers(1,11)); ...; normalize; s=score; if s<best: update; noimp=0
```

Random block function:
```
def random_perturb(a, sigma):
   out=a.copy()
   # mix of block and global
   if rng.random()<0.7:
      width=int(2**rng.integers(1, 11))
      width=min(width, n)
      start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-width+1)))
      out[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma, size=width))
   else:
      out *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma*0.5, size=n))
   if rng.random()<0.2:
      out=0.5*(out+out[::-1])
   return normalize(out)
```
Ensure nonnegative.

LP phase:
```
if linprog is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 40:
   lp_deadline = deadline - 30
   last_m=best_surv
   fails=0
   for it in range(20):
      if time>lp_deadline: break
      c=conv(cur); M=float(c.max())
      topP=min(32,2*n-1)
      idx_top=np.argpartition(c, -topP)[-topP:]
      extra=np.unique(np.linspace(0, 2*n-2, min(32,2*n-1)).astype(int))
      idx=np.unique(np.concatenate([idx_top, extra]))
      Ptot=len(idx)
      A=np.zeros((Ptot+1,n))
      for r,k in enumerate(idx): fill
      A[-1]=1.0
      b=np.full(Ptot, M*1+1e-9?); b[-1]=5*S0
      try:
         res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=[(0,1000.0)]*n, method='highs')
      except Exception:
         fails+=1; if fails>2 break; else continue
      if not res.success: fails+=1; continue
      g=res.x
      sump=g.sum()
      if sump<=1e-9: continue
      g=normalize(g)
      fails=0
      improved=False
      for t in (0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.8):
         cand=(1-t)*cur + t*g
         cand=normalize(cand)
         s=score(cand)
         if s < best_surv -1e-10:
            cur=cand; best_surv=s; improved=True; break
      if not improved:
         fails+=1
         if fails>=3: break
```

```
import numpy as np
import time, math
try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog=None

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    budget=float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 1300.0
    deadline=t0+max(0.0,budget-5.0)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    fallback=[1.0]
    parent=None
    try:
       p=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       if p.size>0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)): parent=p
    except Exception: pass
    if budget < 1.0:
       if parent is not None:
          lst=[float(min(1000.0,max(0.0,x))) for x in parent]
          return lst
       return fallback
    if parent is not None and 64 <= parent.size <= 10000:
       n=int(parent.size)
    else:
       n=2048
    S0=math.sqrt(2.0*float(n))
    conv_out=2*n-1
    fft_n=1
    while fft_n < conv_out: fft_n <<=1
    conv_n=fft_n
    def normalize(a): ...
    def conv(a):
       fa=np.fft.rfft(a, conv_n)
       c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, conv_n, conv_n)
       c=c[:conv_out]
       c=np.real(c)
       return c
    def score(a):
       a=np.asarray(a,float)
       ssum=a.sum()
       if ssum <=0 or not finite: return inf
       c=conv(a)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
          # direct fallback
          cd=np.convolve(a,a)
          mb=float(np.max(cd))
       else:
          mb=float(np.max(np.maximum(c,0.0)))
       return float(2.0*n*mb/(ssum*ssum))
    best_exact_seq=None; best_exact_score=np.inf
    def check_exact(a, reserve=5.0):
       nonlocal best_exact_seq,best_exact_score
       if a is None: return
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - reserve: return
       try:
          lst=[float(min(1000.0,max(0.0,x))) for x in np.asarray(a,float).ravel()]
          if not lst: return
          s=float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
       except Exception:
          s=np.inf
       if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_exact_score:
          best_exact_score=s; best_exact_seq=lst
    # profiles
    iArr=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    denom=n-1 if n>1 else 1
    best_a=None; best_s=np.inf
    # maybe if n large but budget small, reduce param count? Let's use.
    p_vals=(0.4,0.5,0.6,0.7)
    alpha_vals=(0.5,1.0,2.0)
    taper_vals=(0.0,0.1,0.3,0.7)
    add_vals=(0.0,0.05,0.15)
    for p in p_vals:
      if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120: break
      for alpha in alpha_vals:
        base=(iArr+alpha)**(-p)
        for taper in taper_vals:
          if taper:
             b=base*np.exp(-taper*iArr/denom)
          else:
             b=base
          bmax=float(b.max())
          for add in add_vals:
            bb=b if add==0.0 else b + add*bmax
            # sym
            for sym in (False, True):
              if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120: break
              if sym: b2=0.5*(bb+bb[::-1])
              else: b2=bb
              a=normalize(b2)
              s=score(a)
              if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
                 best_s=s; best_a=a.copy()
    if best_a is None:
       best_a=np.full(n, S0/float(n)); best_s=score(best_a)
    # include parent normalized if same length
    if parent is not None and parent.size == n:
       ap=normalize(parent)
       sp=score(ap)
       if np.isfinite(sp) and sp < best_s:
          best_s=sp; best_a=ap.copy()
    # exact check initial and parent
    check_exact(best_a)
    if parent is not None:
       check_exact(parent, reserve=10.0)
    # local
    cur=best_a.copy(); best_surv=best_s
    def random_perturb(a, sigma): ...
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
       P=max(16, min(64, (2*n)//60)) # for 3809 64? 7618/60=127 cap64
       noimp=0
       # mirror loop up to a certain time
       mirror_deadline=deadline - 90.0
       max_iter=500000
       for it in range(max_iter):
          if time.monotonic() > mirror_deadline: break
          c=conv(cur)
          c=np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
          c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
          M=float(c.max())
          if not np.isfinite(M): break
          if conv_out > P:
             idx=np.argpartition(c, -P)[-P:]
          else:
             idx=np.arange(conv_out)
          vals=c[idx]
          diff=M - vals
          smax=float(diff.max())
          if smax > 1e-12:
             w=np.exp(-diff/(0.1*smax + 1e-30))
             wsum=w.sum()
             if wsum>0 and finite: w=w/w.sum()
             else: w=np.full(len(idx), 1.0/len(idx))
          else:
             w=np.full(len(idx), 1.0/len(idx))
          grad=np.zeros(n)
          for kk, ww in zip(idx, w):
             if ww <=0: continue
             if kk < n:
                grad[:kk+1] += ww * cur[kk::-1]
             else:
                st=kk-n+1
                grad[st:] += ww * cur[n-1:st-1:-1]
          grad /= S0
          d=grad - grad.mean()
          scale=float(np.std(d))
          if scale < 1e-14:
             noimp += 1
             if noimp > 200: break
             continue
          base_eta=0.6/scale
          improved=False
          for factor in (1.0,0.35,2.0,0.1):
             eta=min(1000.0, max(1e-5, factor*base_eta))
             cand=normalize(cur * np.exp(-eta * d))
             s=score(cand)
             if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_surv - 1e-11:
                cur=cand; best_surv=s; noimp=0; improved=True
                break
          if not improved:
             noimp += 1
             if noimp > 50 and (noimp % 25 == 0):
                sig=0.05 + 0.3*min(1.0, noimp/1000.0)
                cand=random_perturb(cur, sig)
                s=score(cand)
                if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_surv - 1e-11:
                   cur=cand; best_surv=s; noimp=0
                else:
                   # if not improve, maybe reset noimp? no
                   pass
    # LP phase
    if linprog is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
       lp_deadline=deadline - 40
       fails=0
       for it in range(20):
          if time.monotonic() > lp_deadline: break
          c=conv(cur); c=np.maximum(np.where(finite,c,0),0); M=float(c.max())
          topP=min(32, conv_out)
          if conv_out > topP: top_idx=np.argpartition(c, -topP)[-topP:]
          else: top_idx=np.arange(conv_out)
          extra_count=min(32, conv_out)
          extra=np.unique(np.linspace(0, conv_out-1, extra_count).astype(int))
          idx=np.unique(np.concatenate((top_idx, extra)))
          Ptot=len(idx)
          A=np.zeros((Ptot+1, n))
          for r,k in enumerate(idx): fill
          A[-1]=1.0
          b=np.full(Ptot, M*1.000001+1e-9)
          b[-1]=5.0*S0
          opts={'presolve': True}
          rem=lp_deadline - time.monotonic()
          if rem > 2: opts['time_limit'] = max(1.0, min(30.0, rem))
          try:
             res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=[(0.0,1000.0)]*n, method='highs', options=opts)
          except TypeError:
             res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=[(0.0,1000.0)]*n, method='highs')
          except Exception:
             fails+=1; break
          if not res.success:
             fails+=1
             if fails>=3: break
             continue
          g=res.x
          if g is None: fails+=1; continue
          g=np.asarray(g,float)
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): fails+=1; continue
          sump=float(g.sum())
          if sump <=1e-12: fails+=1; continue
          g=normalize(g)
          improved=False
          for t in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.8):
             cand=(1.0-t)*cur + t*g
             cand=normalize(cand)
             s=score(cand)
             if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_surv - 1e-11:
                cur=cand; best_surv=s; improved=True; break
          if not improved:
             fails+=1
             if fails>=3: break
          else:
             fails=0
    # random phase
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30:
       random_deadline=deadline - 30
       sigma=0.05
       noimp=0
       big_count=0
       while time.monotonic() < random_deadline:
          if noimp > 200:
             sigma *= 1.25
             noimp=0
             big_count+=1
             if big_count>10: break
          cand=random_perturb(cur, sigma)
          s=score(cand)
          if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_surv - 1e-11:
             cur=cand; best_surv=s; noimp=0; sigma=max(0.02, sigma*0.8)
          else:
             noimp+=1
          # occasionally try block gradient step? no
    # final exact candidates
    candidates=[]
    if cur is not None: candidates.append(cur)
    if best_a is not None: candidates.append(best_a)
    if parent is not None: candidates.append(parent)
    candidates.append(np.full(n, S0/float(n)))
    # maybe exact check for candidates if time
    for a in candidates:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
       check_exact(a, reserve=5.0)
    if best_exact_seq is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(best_exact_seq)):
       # last fallback exact [1.0]
       try:
          s=float(evaluate_sequence(fallback))
          if np.isfinite(s): best_exact_seq=fallback; best_exact_score=s
       except Exception:
          best_exact_seq=fallback
    # Ensure final nonempty finite
    out=[]
    try:
       arr=np.asarray(best_exact_seq, dtype=float).ravel()
       if arr.size>0 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
          out=[float(min(1000.0,max(0.0,x))) for x in arr]
       else:
          out=[float(x) for x in fallback]
    except Exception:
       out=[float(x) for x in fallback]
    if not out: out=fallback
    return out
```

```
def normalize(vec):
    arr=np.asarray(vec,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, S0/float(n))
    arr = arr * (S0 / s)
    mx=float(arr.max())
    if mx > 1000.0:
        arr = arr * (1000.0 / mx)
        s2=float(arr.sum())
        if s2 > 1e-300:
            arr = arr * (S0 / s2)
    return arr
```

`d=grad - grad.mean()`. `scale=float(np.std(d))` (population). If scale tiny, random.
Line search: `arg = -eta * d`; clip. `cand=normalize(cur * np.exp(arg))`. `score(cand)` may be slightly lower; update.

- `random_perturb`: width `int(2 ** rng.integers(1, 11))` gives 2^1=2 to 2^10=1024. For n maybe 256; min. Good. Start if n-width+1 <=0? width min n. `rng.integers(0, max(1, n-width+1))` with high exclusive. If n=1? n min 64; fine.
Global: `out *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma*0.5, n))`. Sym: `out=0.5*(out+out[::-1])`.

- LP row fill: `idx` may be sorted by unique. Use `b = np.full(Ptot, M * 1.00001 + 1e-8)`. If M small? ~1.5. Good.
`A[-1] = 1.0`, `b[-1]=5.0*S0`.
`linprog` objective negative. Bounds caps. `res.x` maybe None; check.
If `linprog` not installed? imported try. If method 'highs' unavailable? SciPy linprog defaults to Highs if available. If not, may fallback. We can pass method='highs' inside try; if fails due method, another try without method?
```
try: res=linprog(..., method='highs', options=opts)
except Exception:
  try: res=linprog(...)
  except: fails...
```
Good.
Options time_limit might not be accepted; include in first, if TypeError catch and no opts. But other exceptions? We'll catch broad and retry simpler.

- Random phase: If n large, many random perturb full global size n; cheap. `while time < random_deadline`; no iteration limit but time check. Could be many (e.g., 10k). Good.

- Force exact: Implement after phases:
```
def force_exact(vec):
   nonlocal best_exact_seq,best_exact_score
   if vec is None: return
   if time.monotonic() >= hard_deadline: return
   try...
```
`hard_deadline = t0 + max(0.0, budget - 0.5)`. For budget 1300, headroom 1299.5. We have deadline 1295, 4.5s left. Exact one 3809 ~0.1s; maybe two. Good.
Force candidates: `cur`, `best_a`, parent if size maybe, constant. But exact parent maybe 14M; can use if time. We'll force at most 3. Order: current, best_a, parent. If best_exact already very good? no.
After force, if best_exact_seq is None, force constant [1.0] (n=1) quick. Also ensure fallback.

Potential: `force_exact` of parent with length 3809 okay. If parent length 0 skip.

- Final out: If `best_exact_seq` is not finite, use `cur` if finite? But if cur not exact validated? We can return cur list even if not exact? The contract says verify final, but if time impossible, return finite. We'll do:
```
if best_exact_seq is None:
   best_exact_seq = [float(x) for x in normalize(best_a)] if best_a is not None else fallback
```
Then sanitize.
If exact best is [1.0] but cur surrogate lower unvalidated? If no time, we may return cur? Better return validated if any. But if none validated, return cur (finite) rather than [1.0] because likely better. However contract says use evaluator for final; we should validate one. We'll force exact on [1.0] quick; but if time exhausted, fallback. okay.

Now, if `budget_s` small (e.g., 2s), we set deadline = t0+(-3?) max0=0? `budget-5` negative => deadline t0. Then profile grid may time check `time > deadline - 240` (negative) false? `deadline - 240` negative, time > negative true, so break after first? Actually `time.monotonic() > -240` true, so skip profiles after count? We can add if budget <5 return simple. Let's handle at top:

```
if budget < 5:
   if parent is not None and parent.size>0: return [float...]
   return fallback
```
This respects small budget. For budget 5-10, deadline t0+0 or small; profile maybe skip. Good.
Set min budget for optimization: if budget <50, reduce max_profiles and skip LP/random. We'll implement.
```

Simpler keep parent length; if budget 2s quick return parent. If budget 10s, parent exact 0.1, profiles 80 maybe okay.
```
We'll add:

```
   if parent is not None and parent.size <=5000:
      return list(parent clipped) # score maybe 2 but valid
   else return fallback
```
But perhaps hidden unit tests call with budget_s=100? okay. We don't need optimize for tiny.

Given final answer expected robust for default.

Let's ensure no name conflict `evaluate_sequence` not defined in our namespace? It is global. We call inside functions; okay.

Now, one subtle mathematical point: The evaluator computes `max_b = max(b_sequence)`, where `b_sequence` from `np.convolve` can have dtype float64. For nonnegative a, all coefficients positive; max exact. Our surrogate uses FFT and max of real. Good.

Could we exploit length parent exact to see if our n selection = parent size 3809. If parent is exactly constant, local profile better. Fine.

Let's prepare final code.

Potential missing: In LP, if `idx` sorted includes all? `np.unique` of concatenate top and extra returns sorted; good.

Potential missing: In profile loops with `right = denom - iArr + 1.0`, if q=0, `right ** 0` okay; if q negative? no. If q large, right at end=1; fine.

Potential performance: nested loops recompute `base` for each p,alpha; `base=(iArr+alpha)**(-p)`, and inside q multiply. Good.

Let's write final code now. Ensure only code fenced. We'll not include analysis. final answer code.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 1300.0
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(0.0, budget - 0.5)
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, budget - 5.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    fallback = [1.0]

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    # Very small budgets: return a safe valid candidate immediately.
    if budget < 5.0:
        if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
            out = [float(min(1000.0, max(0.0, float(x)))) for x in parent]
            if out and np.all(np.isfinite(out)):
                return out
        return list(fallback)

    # Use the parent length when available and reasonable, otherwise a compact length.
    if parent is not None and 64 <= parent.size <= 10000:
        n = int(parent.size)
    else:
        n = 2048

    target_sum = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))

    conv_out = 2 * n - 1
    fft_len = 1
    while fft_len < conv_out:
        fft_len <<= 1

    def normalize(vec):
        arr = np.asarray(vec, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            return np.full(n, target_sum / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        arr = arr * (target_sum / s)
        mx = float(arr.max())
        if mx > 1000.0:
            arr = arr * (1000.0 / mx)
            s2 = float(arr.sum())
            if s2 > 1e-300:
                arr = arr * (target_sum / s2)
        return arr

    def conv_fft(vec):
        a = np.asarray(vec, dtype=np.float64)
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_len)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, fft_len)
        return c[:conv_out]

    def surrogate_score(vec):
        a = np.asarray(vec, dtype=np.float64)
        ssum = float(a.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <= 1e-300:
            return np.inf
        try:
            c = conv_fft(a)
            c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
            mb = float(np.max(np.maximum(c, 0.0)))
        except Exception:
            try:
                cd = np.convolve(a, a)
                mb = float(np.max(cd))
            except Exception:
                return np.inf
        if not np.isfinite(mb):
            return np.inf
        return float((2.0 * n * mb) / (ssum * ssum))

    best_exact_seq = None
    best_exact_score = np.inf

    def update_exact(vals):
        nonlocal best_exact_seq, best_exact_score
        if vals is None:
            return
        if time.monotonic() >= hard_deadline:
            return
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            lst = [float(min(1000.0, max(0.0, x))) for x in arr]
            if not lst:
                return
            s = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
        except Exception:
            s = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_exact_score:
            best_exact_score = s
            best_exact_seq = lst

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Profile generation: nonnegative curves concentrated toward one side,
    # with modest right-side taper and endpoint smoothing.  With the expert
    # normalization sum(a) = sqrt(2n), the exact score equals max(a*a).
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    iArr = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    denom = float(n - 1) if n > 1 else 1.0
    right_base = denom - iArr + 1.0

    p_vals = (0.40, 0.50, 0.60, 0.70, 0.80, 1.00)
    alpha_vals = (0.50, 1.00, 2.00, 4.00)
    q_vals = (0.00, 0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.80)
    add_vals = (0.00, 0.05, 0.15)

    max_profiles = 720 if budget > 200 else (240 if budget > 50 else 60)
    profile_time_stop = min(deadline, t0 + max(30.0, 0.25 * budget))

    best_a = None
    best_s = np.inf
    profile_count = 0

    for p in p_vals:
        if profile_count >= max_profiles or time.monotonic() > profile_time_stop:
            break
        for alpha in alpha_vals:
            if profile_count >= max_profiles or time.monotonic() > profile_time_stop:
                break
            left = iArr + alpha
            for q in q_vals:
                if profile_count >= max_profiles or time.monotonic() > profile_time_stop:
                    break
                base = left ** (-p)
                if q != 0.0:
                    base = base * (right_base ** (-q))
                bmax = float(base.max())
                for add in add_vals:
                    if profile_count >= max_profiles or time.monotonic() > profile_time_stop:
                        break
                    bb = base if add == 0.0 else base + (add * bmax)
                    for sym in (False, True):
                        if profile_count >= max_profiles:
                            break
                        if sym:
                            b2 = 0.5 * (bb + bb[::-1])
                        else:
                            b2 = bb
                        profile_count += 1
                        a = normalize(b2)
                        s = surrogate_score(a)
                        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
                            best_s = s
                            best_a = a.copy()

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.full(n, target_sum / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        best_s = surrogate_score(best_a)
        if not np.isfinite(best_s):
            best_s = 2.0

    # Include parent normalized to the expert scale, if its length matches n.
    if parent is not None and parent.size == n:
        pn = normalize(parent)
        ps = surrogate_score(pn)
        if np.isfinite(ps) and ps < best_s:
            best_s = ps
            best_a = pn.copy()

    # Validate at least one candidate exactly before expensive search.
    update_exact(best_a)
    if parent is not None:
        update_exact(parent)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Multiplicative mirror-style local search on the current max lags.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    cur = best_a.copy()
    best_surv = best_s

    def random_perturb(a, sigma):
        out = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        u = rng.random()
        if u < 0.65:
            width = int(2 ** rng.integers(1, 11))
            width = max(1, min(width, n))
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
            out[start:start + width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, width))
        elif u < 0.90:
            out *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5 * sigma, n))
        else:
            w = int(2 ** rng.integers(1, 8))
            w = max(1, min(w, n // 2 if n > 2 else 1))
            s1 = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w + 1)))
            s2 = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w + 1)))
            f1 = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
            f2 = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
            out[s1:s1 + w] *= f1
            out[s2:s2 + w] *= f2
        if rng.random() < 0.2:
            out = 0.5 * (out + out[::-1])
        return normalize(out)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
        P = max(16, min(64, int(round((2.0 * n) / 60.0))))
        mirror_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + max(60.0, 0.55 * budget))
        no_improve = 0

        for _ in range(1000000):
            if time.monotonic() > mirror_deadline:
                break

            c = conv_fft(cur)
            c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
            c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
            M = float(c.max())
            if not np.isfinite(M):
                break

            if conv_out > P:
                idx = np.argpartition(c, -P)[-P:]
            else:
                idx = np.arange(conv_out)

            vals = c[idx]
            diff = M - vals
            smax = float(diff.max())
            if smax > 1e-12:
                w = np.exp(-diff / (0.1 * smax + 1e-300))
                wsum = float(w.sum())
                if np.isfinite(wsum) and wsum > 0.0:
                    w = w / wsum
                else:
                    w = np.full(len(idx), 1.0 / len(idx), dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                w = np.full(len(idx), 1.0 / len(idx), dtype=np.float64)

            grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            for kk, ww in zip(idx, w):
                if ww <= 0.0:
                    continue
                kk = int(kk)
                if kk < n:
                    grad[:kk + 1] += ww * cur[kk::-1]
                else:
                    st = kk - n + 1
                    grad[st:] += ww * cur[n - 1:st - 1:-1]

            grad /= target_sum
            d = grad - float(grad.mean())
            scale = float(np.std(d))
            if scale < 1e-14:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve > 250:
                    break
                continue

            base_eta = 0.6 / scale
            improved = False
            for factor in (1.0, 0.35, 2.0, 0.1):
                eta = min(1000.0, max(1e-5, factor * base_eta))
                arg = -eta * d
                arg = np.clip(arg, -20.0, 20.0)
                cand = normalize(cur * np.exp(arg))
                s = surrogate_score(cand)
                if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_surv - 1e-11:
                    cur = cand
                    best_surv = s
                    no_improve = 0
                    improved = True
                    break

            if not improved:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve > 40 and (no_improve % 30 == 0):
                    sig = 0.05 + 0.30 * min(1.0, no_improve / 1000.0)
                    cand = random_perturb(cur, sig)
                    s = surrogate_score(cand)
                    if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_surv - 1e-11:
                        cur = cand
                        best_surv = s
                        no_improve = 0

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Optional active-constraint LP direction changes, in the parent LP spirit.
    # Only active and cover lags are used to keep the LP small.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    if linprog is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
        lp_deadline = deadline - 40.0
        fails = 0

        for _ in range(24):
            if time.monotonic() > lp_deadline:
                break

            c = conv_fft(cur)
            c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
            c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
            M = float(c.max())
            if not np.isfinite(M):
                break

            topP = min(32, conv_out)
            if conv_out > topP:
                top_idx = np.argpartition(c, -topP)[-topP:]
            else:
                top_idx = np.arange(conv_out)

            extra_count = min(32, conv_out)
            extra_idx = np.unique(np.linspace(0, conv_out - 1, extra_count).astype(int))
            idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((np.asarray(top_idx, dtype=int), extra_idx)))
            Ptot = len(idx)

            A = np.zeros((Ptot + 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
            for r, kk in enumerate(idx):
                kk = int(kk)
                if kk < n:
                    A[r, :kk + 1] = cur[kk::-1]
                else:
                    st = kk - n + 1
                    A[r, st:] = cur[n - 1:st - 1:-1]
            A[-1, :] = 1.0

            b = np.full(Ptot, M * 1.00001 + 1e-8, dtype=np.float64)
            b[-1] = 5.0 * target_sum

            opts = {"presolve": True}
            rem = lp_deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem > 2.0:
                opts["time_limit"] = max(1.0, min(30.0, rem))

            try:
                res = linprog(
                    -np.ones(n),
                    A_ub=A,
                    b_ub=b,
                    bounds=[(0.0, 1000.0)] * n,
                    method="highs",
                    options=opts,
                )
            except Exception:
                try:
                    res = linprog(
                        -np.ones(n),
                        A_ub=A,
                        b_ub=b,
                        bounds=[(0.0, 1000.0)] * n,
                        method="highs",
                    )
                except Exception:
                    fails += 1
                    break

            if not res.success:
                fails += 1
                if fails >= 3:
                    break
                continue

            g = res.x
            if g is None:
                fails += 1
                continue
            g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
                fails += 1
                continue
            gs = float(g.sum())
            if gs <= 1e-12:
                fails += 1
                continue

            g = normalize(g)
            improved = False
            for t in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.50, 0.80):
                cand = (1.0 - t) * cur + t * g
                cand = normalize(cand)
                s = surrogate_score(cand)
                if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_surv - 1e-11:
                    cur = cand
                    best_surv = s
                    improved = True
                    break

            if improved:
                fails = 0
            else:
                fails += 1
                if fails >= 3:
                    break

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Random block / global polishing.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        random_deadline = deadline - 30.0
        sigma = 0.05
        no_improve = 0
        grows = 0

        while time.monotonic() < random_deadline:
            if no_improve > 250:
                sigma *= 1.25
                no_improve = 0
                grows += 1
                if grows > 12:
                    break
                if sigma > 0.9:
                    sigma = 0.05

            cand = random_perturb(cur, sigma)
            s = surrogate_score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_surv - 1e-11:
                cur = cand
                best_surv = s
                no_improve = 0
                sigma = max(0.02, sigma * 0.80)
            else:
                no_improve += 1

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final exact validation of the best candidates.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    update_exact(cur)
    update_exact(best_a)
    if parent is not None:
        update_exact(parent)

    # A guaranteed valid fallback, checked if necessary.
    if best_exact_seq is None:
        update_exact(np.full(n, target_sum / float(n)))

    if best_exact_seq is None:
        update_exact(fallback)

    try:
        arr = np.asarray(best_exact_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel() if best_exact_seq is not None else None
        if arr is not None and arr.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            out = [float(min(1000.0, max(0.0, x))) for x in arr]
        else:
            out = [float(x) for x in fallback]
    except Exception:
        out = [float(x) for x in fallback]

    if not out:
        out = list(fallback)

    return out
```
